# Summary

Every failed request returns an HTTP 4xx or 5xx status and a JSON body in the failure envelope (see [Response envelope](response-envelope.md)):

```json
{
  "status": "failure",
  "summary": "SECURITY_NOT_PERMITTED",
  "data": {
    "errorCode": 7301,
    "errorMessage": "You (<user-name>) do not have the permission to do this operation. "
  }
}
```

How to use this catalog:

- Match on `data.errorCode` (integer), never on `errorMessage` text, which is localized and may change.
- `summary` carries a stable upper-case constant for many codes; the known constants are listed per code below.
- The HTTP status column says "observed" when a documented sample response shows the status, and "typical" when it is inferred from the code family (401 for token errors, 403 for permission errors, 404 for missing objects, 500 for server errors, 400 for everything else).
- The same code can carry an operation-specific meaning; the "Raised by" table lists each documented reason and solution per operation.
- Retry guidance: 5xx and concurrency guards (for example a job or copy already in progress) are retryable after a delay; 4xx codes are not retryable without changing the request.

# Code Families

| Range | Family | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| 6xxx | Organization, user and role management | Membership, roles, portal domains. |
| 7xxx | Metadata, security and modeling | 7103/7104 missing objects, 7301 permission denied, 73xx sharing, 74xx query tables and data lock, 75xx criteria and formula validation, 78xx export/import. |
| 8xxx | Request validation, jobs and platform limits | 80xx invalid CONFIG and parameters, 81xx import/export jobs and limits, 82xx system tags, 85xx OAuth and parameter framework. |
| 9xxx, 12xxx, 14xxx, 15xxx, 18xxx, 70xxx, 101xxx | Feature specific | Localization (9102), white label (12xxx), scheduling and datasource sync (18xxx), AutoML (70xxx), stream tables (101021). |

# Codes

## Error 6004

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `6004` |
| Summary constant | Not documented |
| HTTP status | 400 (typical) |
| Meaning | Adding these users would exceed the organisation's user seat limit under the current plan. |
| Resolution | Upgrade the plan to accommodate more users, or remove unused user accounts before adding new ones. |
| Retryable | No, fix the request first |

Raised by:

| Operation(s) | Reason | Solution |
|---|---|---|
| [Add Users](../domains/users-and-groups/org-users/add-users.md) | Adding these users would exceed the organisation's user seat limit under the current plan. | Upgrade the plan to accommodate more users, or remove unused user accounts before adding new ones. |
| [Activate Users](../domains/users-and-groups/org-users/activate-users.md) | Activating these users would exceed the organisation's user seat limit. | Check current user count via Get Resource Details. Upgrade the plan or remove unused users before reactivating. |

| [Create Slide Show](../domains/share-and-publish/slideshow-management/create-slideshow.md) | `PUBLISHCNT_VIOLATION` — `accessType: 1` requested but the plan does not allow login-free (private-link style) access. | Use `accessType: 0`, or upgrade the plan. |

| Summary constant | Not documented |
| HTTP status | 400 (typical) |
| Meaning | Malformed URL — the ID is not a valid numeric path segment. |

| Operation(s) | Reason | Solution |

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `7092` |
| Summary constant | `DDL_LOCK_SINCE_IMPORT_IN_PROGRESS` |
| HTTP status | 400 (typical) |
| Meaning | DDLLOCKSINCEIMPORTINPROGRESS — A batch import is holding a lock in this workspace. |
| Resolution | Retry once it has finished. |
| Retryable | No, fix the request first |

| Operation(s) | Reason | Solution |

| [Add Column](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/columns/add-column.md) | DDL lock is active — an import or schema operation is in progress on the table. | Wait for the current import or schema operation to complete before adding a column. |

| [Batch Import Data into New Table](../domains/data-operations/async-data-import/batch-import-new-table.md), [Create Import Job for a New Table (Asynchronous)](../domains/data-operations/async-data-import/create-import-job-new-table.md), [Import Data into a New Table (Synchronous)](../domains/data-operations/sync-data-import/import-data-new-table.md) | `DDL_LOCK_SINCE_IMPORT_IN_PROGRESS` — A batch import is holding a lock in this workspace. | Retry once it has finished. |

## Error 7104

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `7104` |
| Summary constant | `META_OBJECT_NOT_PRESENT` |
| HTTP status | 404 (typical) |
| Meaning | The view (table, report, dashboard, query table) or other named object addressed by the request does not exist in the given workspace. |
| Resolution | Verify the view-id or the exact, case-sensitive name used in the request. |
| Retryable | No, fix the request first |

Raised by:

| Operation(s) | Reason | Solution |
|---|---|---|
| [Get Meta Details From Name](../domains/organization-management/org-info-and-settings/get-meta-details.md) | Workspace or view not found by the provided name. | Verify the exact spelling and case of `workspaceName` and `viewName`. Names are case-sensitive. |

## Error 7105

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `7105` |
| Summary constant | Not documented |
| HTTP status | 400 (typical) |
| Meaning | The specified view does not exist. |
| Resolution | Verify the <view-id> using the Get View List API. |
| Retryable | No, fix the request first |

| Operation(s) | Reason | Solution |
|---|---|---|

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `7106` |
| Summary constant | `META_OBJECT_NOT_PRESENT` |
| HTTP status | 404 (observed in sample responses) |
| Meaning | The report does not exist or has been deleted. |

| Operation(s) | Reason | Solution |

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `7107` |
| Summary constant | `META_OBJECT_NOT_PRESENT` |
| HTTP status | 400 (observed in sample responses) |
| Meaning | The specified column does not exist in the table. |

| [Hide Columns](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/columns/hide-columns.md), [Show Columns](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/columns/show-columns.md) | One or more column IDs do not exist in the table. | Verify all IDs using Get Table Metadata. |
| [Add Lookup](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/lookups-and-relationships/add-lookup.md), [Remove Lookup](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/lookups-and-relationships/remove-lookup.md) | The specified child column (`<column-id>`) does not exist in the child table. | Verify `<column-id>` using Get Table Metadata on the child view. |
| [Add Lookup](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/lookups-and-relationships/add-lookup.md), [Remove Lookup](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/lookups-and-relationships/remove-lookup.md) | The specified child column (`<column-id>`) does not exist in the child table. | Verify `<column-id>` using Get Table Metadata. |
| [Delete Custom Formula](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/formula-columns/delete-formula-column.md) | The specified column ID does not exist in the view. | Verify `<formula-id>` using Get Custom Formulas. |
| [Delete Aggregate Formula](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/aggregate-formulas/delete-aggregate-formula.md) | The specified formula ID does not exist as a column on this view. | Verify `<formula-id>` using Get Aggregate Formula. |
| [Create Import Job for an Existing Table (Asynchronous)](../domains/data-operations/async-data-import/create-import-job-existing-table.md), [Import Data into an Existing Table (Synchronous)](../domains/data-operations/sync-data-import/import-data-existing-table.md) | `META_OBJECT_NOT_PRESENT` — A column named in `matchingColumns` is not present in the table. | Verify the names via [Get Columns](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/columns/overview.md). |
| [Batch Import Data into Existing Table](../domains/data-operations/async-data-import/batch-import-existing-table.md) | `META_OBJECT_NOT_PRESENT` — A column named in `matchingColumns` is not present. | Verify the names via [Get Columns](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/columns/overview.md). |
| [Auto Analyse Column](../domains/views-management/auto-analysis/auto-analyse-column.md) | Column not found in the specified table. | Verify `<column-id>` belongs to the table identified by `<view-id>`. |

| [Disable Workspace for Domain Access](../domains/workspace-management/domain-and-white-label/disable-domain-workspace.md) | The caller is not an Account Admin or Organization Admin of the workspace's organisation, or the organisation has no White Label domain. | Ensure the caller has admin access and the org has a configured White Label domain. |

## Error 7307

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `7307` |
| Summary constant | `OWNER_CANNOT_SHARE_HIMSELF` |
| HTTP status | 400 (typical) |
| Meaning | OWNERCANNOTSHAREHIMSELF — The sharer attempted to share a view to themselves. |
| Resolution | Remove the sharer's own email from emailIds. |
| Retryable | No, fix the request first |

Raised by:

| Operation(s) | Reason | Solution |
|---|---|---|
| [Share Views](../domains/share-and-publish/sharing/share-views.md) | `OWNER_CANNOT_SHARE_HIMSELF` — The sharer attempted to share a view to themselves. | Remove the sharer's own email from `emailIds`. |

## Error 7309

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `7309` |
| Summary constant | `SECURITY_NEEDS_LOGIN` |
| HTTP status | 400 (typical) |
| Meaning | SECURITYNEEDSLOGIN — No authentication was supplied. |
| Resolution | Send an Authorization header. |
| Retryable | No, fix the request first |

Raised by:

| Operation(s) | Reason | Solution |
|---|---|---|
| [Create Custom Role](../domains/users-and-groups/custom-roles/create-custom-role.md), [Delete Custom Role](../domains/users-and-groups/custom-roles/delete-custom-role.md), [Get Custom Roles](../domains/users-and-groups/custom-roles/get-custom-roles.md), [Update Custom Role](../domains/users-and-groups/custom-roles/update-custom-role.md) | `SECURITY_NEEDS_LOGIN` — No authentication was supplied. | Send an `Authorization` header. |
| [Create Dashboard](../domains/reports-and-dashboards/dashboards/create-dashboard.md), [Read Dashboard Metadata](../domains/reports-and-dashboards/dashboards/get-dashboard-metadata.md), [Update Dashboard](../domains/reports-and-dashboards/dashboards/update-dashboard.md) | `Authorization` header absent. | Send `Authorization: Zoho-oauthtoken <token>`. |
| [Create Dashboard](../domains/reports-and-dashboards/dashboards/create-dashboard.md), [Read Dashboard Metadata](../domains/reports-and-dashboards/dashboards/get-dashboard-metadata.md), [Update Dashboard](../domains/reports-and-dashboards/dashboards/update-dashboard.md) | `Authorization` header absent. | Send the OAuth header. |

## Error 7319

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `7319` |
| Summary constant | `OBJID_NOT_BELONGS_TO_DB` |
| HTTP status | 400 (typical) |
| Meaning | The view does not belong to the specified workspace. |
| Resolution | Ensure <workspace-id> and <view-id> are consistent. |
| Retryable | No, fix the request first |

Raised by:

| Operation(s) | Reason | Solution |
|---|---|---|
| [Export as Template](../domains/workspace-management/workspace-operations/export-as-template.md) | One or more specified view IDs do not belong to the specified workspace. | Ensure all `viewIds` belong to the workspace identified by `<workspace-id>`. |
| [Move Views To Folder](../domains/workspace-management/workspace-folders/move-views-to-folder.md) | One or more view IDs do not belong to the specified workspace. | Ensure all view IDs in `viewIds` belong to the workspace identified by `<workspace-id>`. |
| [Get Table Metadata](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/table-and-schema/get-table-metadata.md) | The specified view ID does not belong to the given workspace. | Confirm the `<view-id>` belongs to the workspace identified by `<workspace-id>`. |
| [Get Column Dependents](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/columns/get-column-dependents.md), [Reorder Columns](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/columns/reorder-columns.md), [Sort Data by Columns](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/columns/sort-data-by-columns.md) | The view ID does not belong to the specified workspace. | Confirm the `<view-id>` belongs to the workspace in the URL. |
| [Get Column Dependents](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/columns/get-column-dependents.md), [Reorder Columns](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/columns/reorder-columns.md), [Sort Data by Columns](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/columns/sort-data-by-columns.md) | The view ID does not belong to the specified workspace. | Confirm the `<view-id>` is correct. |
| [Add Lookup](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/lookups-and-relationships/add-lookup.md) | The reference table (`referenceViewId`) does not belong to the same workspace as the child table. | Both tables must be in the workspace identified by `<workspace-id>`. |

## Error 8080

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `8080` |
| Summary constant | `INVALID_JSON_CONFIGURATION` |
| HTTP status | 400 (typical) |
| Meaning | CONFIG is not valid JSON, was not URL-encoded correctly, contains an unsupported key, or violates a type or length constraint. |
| Resolution | Stringify and URL-encode the CONFIG object and send only the documented keys with the documented types. |
| Retryable | No, fix the request first |

| Operation(s) | Reason | Solution |
|---|---|---|
| [Remove Shared Views](../domains/share-and-publish/sharing/remove-share.md), [Share Views](../domains/share-and-publish/sharing/share-views.md), [Update Shared Details](../domains/share-and-publish/sharing/update-shared-details-for-view.md) | `INVALID_JSON_CONFIGURATION` — CONFIG is malformed or missing a required field. | Validate the CONFIG JSON against the parameter table above. |

| [Make View Public](../domains/share-and-publish/publish/make-views-public.md) | `INVALID_JSON_CONFIGURATION` — CONFIG is not valid JSON, contains an unsupported key, or violates a type/length/allowed-value constraint (e.g. `publicPermLevel` outside `1`/`2`/`3`). | Send only the documented keys with the documented types. |
| [Get Private URL](../domains/share-and-publish/publish/get-private-url.md) | `INVALID_JSON_CONFIGURATION` — CONFIG is not valid JSON or contains an unsupported key. | Send only `domainName` and/or `withCustomDomain`. |
| [Create Private URL](../domains/share-and-publish/publish/create-private-url.md) | `INVALID_JSON_CONFIGURATION` — CONFIG is not valid JSON, contains an unsupported key, or violates a constraint (e.g. `password` shorter than 6 characters, malformed `expiryDate`). | Send only the documented keys with the documented types and formats. |
| [Update Publish Configurations](../domains/share-and-publish/publish/update-publish-configurations.md) | `INVALID_JSON_CONFIGURATION` — CONFIG is not valid JSON, contains an unsupported key, or violates a type constraint (e.g. a non-alphabetic `legendPosition`). | Send only the documented keys with the documented types. |
| [Get Embed URL](../domains/share-and-publish/embed-url/get-embed-url.md) | `INVALID_JSON_CONFIGURATION` — CONFIG is not valid JSON, was not URL-encoded correctly, contains an unsupported key, or violates a type/length constraint. | Stringify and URL-encode the CONFIG object, and send only the documented keys. |
| [Fetch All Embed URLs](../domains/share-and-publish/embed-url/get-embed-urls.md), [Get Slide URL](../domains/share-and-publish/slideshow-management/get-slideshow-url.md) | `INVALID_JSON_CONFIGURATION` — CONFIG is not valid JSON, was not URL-encoded correctly, or contains an unsupported key. | Send only `includeExpiredUrls`, stringified and URL-encoded. |
| [Delete Embed URL](../domains/share-and-publish/embed-url/delete-embed-url.md) | `INVALID_JSON_CONFIGURATION` — CONFIG is missing, is not valid JSON, contains an unsupported key, or `rsConfig` exceeds 1,000 characters. | Send a valid CONFIG containing only `rsConfig` or `deleteAllUrls`. |
| [Get Slide URL](../domains/share-and-publish/slideshow-management/get-slideshow-url.md) | `INVALID_JSON_CONFIGURATION` — CONFIG is not valid JSON, was not URL-encoded correctly, or contains an unsupported key. | Stringify and URL-encode the CONFIG object, and send only the documented keys. |
| [Create Slide Show](../domains/share-and-publish/slideshow-management/create-slideshow.md), [Update Slide Show](../domains/share-and-publish/slideshow-management/update-slideshow.md) | `INVALID_JSON_CONFIGURATION` — CONFIG is not valid JSON, contains an unsupported key, or violates a constraint (e.g. punctuation in `slideName`, more than 100 entries in `viewIds`). | Send only the documented keys with the documented types and value constraints. |

## Error 8120

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `8120` |
| Summary constant | `EXPORT_JOB_NOT_FOUND` |
| HTTP status | 404 (observed in sample responses) |
| Meaning | EXPORTJOBNOTFOUND — No export job exists for the given ID (HTTP 404). |

| Operation(s) | Reason | Solution |

| [Download Exported Data](../domains/data-operations/async-data-export/download-exported-data.md) | `EXPORT_JOB_NOT_FOUND` — No export job exists for the given ID, or it has passed its 72-hour retention (HTTP 404). | Verify the `jobId`; if it has expired, create a new job. |

|---|---|

| Summary constant | `PATTERN_NOT_MATCHED` |
| HTTP status | 400 (typical) |
| Meaning | PATTERNNOTMATCHED — roleName contains disallowed characters, or accessType is not one of the three values. |

| Operation(s) | Reason | Solution |

| [Update Datasource Connection](../domains/data-operations/data-sync-and-connectivity/update-datasource-connection.md) | `PATTERN_NOT_MATCHED` — `serviceName` or `databaseType` is not one of the accepted values. | Send a value from [`serviceName` values](../domains/data-operations/data-sync-and-connectivity/update-datasource-connection.md#servicename-values). |

| [Create Dashboard](../domains/reports-and-dashboards/dashboards/create-dashboard.md), [Update Dashboard](../domains/reports-and-dashboards/dashboards/update-dashboard.md) | An enumerated or pattern-constrained value does not match — a malformed colour, an out-of-range number, `layoutType` outside 1–4, a non-string colour. | Check the ranges in [Themes](../domains/reports-and-dashboards/dashboards/create-dashboard.md#themes) and [Settings](../domains/reports-and-dashboards/dashboards/create-dashboard.md#settings). |

## Error 8539

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `8539` |
| Summary constant | `INVALID_VALUE_NOT_ALLOWED` |
| HTTP status | 400 (typical) |
| Meaning | INVALIDVALUENOTALLOWED — An attribute carries a value that is structurally valid but not accepted, such as an empty roleName. |
| Resolution | Send a non-empty value. |
| Retryable | No, fix the request first |

Raised by:

| Operation(s) | Reason | Solution |
|---|---|---|
| [Create Custom Role](../domains/users-and-groups/custom-roles/create-custom-role.md) | `INVALID_VALUE_NOT_ALLOWED` — An attribute carries a value that is structurally valid but not accepted, such as an empty `roleName`. | Send a non-empty value. |

## Error 8542

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `8542` |
| Summary constant | `CONFIG` |
| HTTP status | 400 (typical) |
| Meaning | An unknown key is present in CONFIG, or a windowFunction is mis-configured. |
| Resolution | Remove the key; check windowFunction.type against the list. |
| Retryable | No, fix the request first |

Raised by:

| Operation(s) | Reason | Solution |
|---|---|---|
| [Create Report](../domains/reports-and-dashboards/reports/create-report.md) | An unknown key is present in `CONFIG`, or a `windowFunction` is mis-configured. | Remove the key; check `windowFunction.type` against [the list](../domains/reports-and-dashboards/reports/create-report.md#window-functions). |
| [Update Report](../domains/reports-and-dashboards/reports/update-report.md) | `baseTableName`, `drillActionConfig`, `modifiedPaths` or any other key outside the Update schema is present. | Remove the key. |
| [Create Dashboard](../domains/reports-and-dashboards/dashboards/create-dashboard.md) | An unknown key is present in `CONFIG`. | Only `displayName`, `description`, `layout`, `themes`, `settings`, `layoutType` are accepted. |
| [Update Dashboard](../domains/reports-and-dashboards/dashboards/update-dashboard.md) | `displayName`, `description`, or any other key outside `layout` / `themes` / `settings` / `layoutType` is present in `CONFIG`. | Remove the key. Renaming is not supported here. |

## Error 8544

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `8544` |
| Summary constant | `OUT_OF_RANGE` |
| HTTP status | 400 (typical) |
| Meaning | OUTOFRANGE — A schedule value is outside its declared range. |
| Resolution | Correct the value. |
| Retryable | No, fix the request first |

Raised by:

| Operation(s) | Reason | Solution |
|---|---|---|
| [Create AutoML Analysis Deployment](../domains/dsml/automl/create-auto-ml-analysis-deployment.md) | `OUT_OF_RANGE` — A schedule value is outside its declared range. | Correct the value. |

## Error 8547
